Tournament hosting remains available as a secondary path through one concise pilot card. The director-first Tournament OS architecture still stands behind the product, but the public page no longer asks a new spectator to understand ledgers, state machines, or control-room operations before they can watch.

- **Website experiments get a shared success loop, and investors get an honest vision board** (2026-07-25). The landing hero copy test is now joined by a landing community-CTA test and an investor-thesis test, all using the shared `app/js/experiments.js` primitive. It records one unique-session impression per arm, excludes query-forced QA views, waits until the below-fold community CTA is actually visible before counting exposure, and records unique-session clicks plus raw target clicks. `/api/log-event` dual-writes bounded rows to `experiment_events`; `/api/admin/experiments` rolls them into views, clicks, and CTR, and Mission Control shows a directional lead only after every arm reaches 20 impressions. The `/investors` page remains indexed in the sitemap but is intentionally absent from the global More menu, keeping the public navigation focused on debaters. It deliberately separates Live now, Building, Long term, and Exploratory ideas, and says plainly that the page is a long-term vision board, not a roadmap, forecast, or offer to invest.

- **Debatable becomes the sole public name** (2026-07-25). Aidan explicitly removed the retired brand everywhere public. The sweep deletes it from visible copy, CTA language, accessibility labels, JSON-LD aliases, social metadata, narration labels, extension copy, and native App Store strings. The main timed-round URL moves to `/practice`; the comparison pages move to `/compare/debatable-vs-*`; the differentiator page moves to `/why-debatable`. Old public URLs remain only as 301 redirects so existing links do not break. Compatibility-only infrastructure may retain legacy domain aliases, external service slugs, the existing iOS bundle identifier, and recognition of the first native build's user-agent token. Those are not brand surfaces and must not leak into copy.

- **Profile becomes a training room, with the debate brain as the first move** (2026-07-25). `/profile` now opens as a personal training surface instead of a stack of equally weighted dashboard cards. The identity hero has more depth and energy, and the first interactive block is a live-fact brain card that shows the user's round, format, and training-day signals. Its primary action links directly to `/spectate#brain`, where Identity, Case vault, and Thinking map are built. Daily quests and the nearest milestone remain beside it, while XP, ballots, mastery, achievements, saved rounds, the generated style fingerprint, and account settings keep their existing data and behavior. The zero-round state gets the same brain entry point, so the compounding loop is visible before the first debate.

- **Email/password joins Google account auth** (2026-07-25). The shared sign-in modal now offers first-class email/password registration and sign-in on web, with display-name capture, an 8-character password minimum, verification email, generic password-reset responses, readable Firebase error states, and anonymous-account linking so a guest's local session is not discarded during registration. Google remains a one-tap option, and Apple remains in the native chooser. The topbar, profile, cross-page signup nudge, `/app`, and `/practice` all route into the same chooser. Firebase Identity Platform is configured for password-required email auth; magic links stay retired.

- **Domain cutover: debateai.com → itsdebatable.com** (2026-07-22). Aidan bought **itsdebatable.com** on the GoDaddy aftermarket (whois: registrar GoDaddy, updated 2026-07-18, registration through 2034 — verified BEFORE the sweep, the step both the 2026-05-25 and 2026-06-08 traps skipped) and asked for it to become the primary domain, with debateai.com and the rest redirecting to it. The domain finally matches the "Debatable" brand. **Code sweep (~160 files):** every `debateai.com` reference flipped to `itsdebatable.com` — canonical tags, og:url/twitter, JSON-LD `url`, sitemap.xml + sitemap index, robots.txt, function site origins (create-checkout `siteUrl` default, OpenRouter HTTP-Referer, email template links), /topics + /compare SEO pages. **netlify.toml (both mirrors):** the 2026-07-18 rules that 301'd itsdebatable.com → debateai.com were INVERTED into a canonicalization block at the top of the redirect chain: www/http variants of itsdebatable.com normalize to the https apex; debateai.com, debateit.live (the interim Netlify primary), debatetable.com, and debatethedevil.com all 301 to itsdebatable.com; debateai.com `/api/*` uses **308** (method-preserving) so stale service-worker-cached pages on the old origin can still POST mid-migration; `debateai.com/native` serves IN PLACE (200 rewrite, no redirect) because the iOS binary uploaded to ASC 2026-07-18 hardcodes debateai.com/native with an allowNavigation list that lacks itsdebatable.com — remove that exemption only after a new binary ships (mobile/capacitor.config.ts already points at itsdebatable.com/native). **CORS:** itsdebatable.com + www ADDED to all 16 allowlist files (lib/response.mjs + per-function copies + root mirrors); debateai.com deliberately KEPT for the migration window. **Deliberately NOT touched:** the Chrome extensions (`app/extension/`, `app/float-extension/` — ship through the store separately; their iframe/API calls ride the 301/308), TWA dir (Play-store coupled), `debateai-*` localStorage keys / CSS classes / JS identifiers, `debateai.daily.co` (external Daily.co subdomain), competitor `debateai.org`, sender email addresses (env-driven; links inside emails DID flip). **Infra moves alongside the code:** GoDaddy DNS pointed at Netlify (A @ → 75.2.60.5, www CNAME → debateos1.netlify.app — the previous owner's Dreamhost nameservers had to go, Forwarding stays OFF per the 2026-07-01 outage), Netlify primary flipped www.debateit.live → itsdebatable.com with all legacy domains kept as aliases, SSL provisioned, itsdebatable.com added to Firebase Auth authorized domains (App Check needs nothing — the site key was never filled in). The push was gated on DNS actually resolving first; code never ships pointing at a dead domain. **Open follow-ups:** GSC property for itsdebatable.com + sitemap submission; Stripe webhook endpoint URL update (Stripe doesn't follow redirects; harmless while checkout is BETA_NO_CHARGE-gated); social handles unchanged.

- **Stated mission on the landing: democratize the verdict, the people decide who won** (2026-07-20). Two new landing sections give the site an explicit "what you do" and "why this exists", distilled from Aidan's July 2026 correspondence with an essayist he had pitched by declaring the essay dead. **`#how-it-works`** sits after the live band: four stations (pick a motion, speak on the clock, it argues back, read your ballot) plus the round CTA. Its "Watch the walkthrough" button is rendered hidden behind a `HOWTO_VIDEO` constant and only appears when that holds a URL, because the founder intro video was pulled 2026-05-19 and nothing replaced it. See the standing no-demo-links rule; this is how it gets satisfied without a dead CTA. **`#why-this-exists`** sits after `#live-proof` and carries the argument: judging, not talent, is the bottleneck ($60-90/hr judges, rounds rationed, 23,000+ US schools with no program, so a shortage of verdicts rather than arguers); the essay is alive as a tool but **dying as a receipt** now that AI made polished prose cheap, which is why schools are returning to oral exams; and argument is a spectator sport whose reputation is earned across hundreds of public rounds rather than one tab room's opinion on one weekend. **TRUTH BOUNDARY, and it is load-bearing: audience voting does not exist.** There is no poll or crowd-vote path in `live-round.html` or `spectate.html`, and The Floor's crowd read was switched off the same day (see the floor preview entry). Spectating IS real, instant ballots ARE real, so tenet 03 says "Next comes the vote" in future tense on purpose. Do not promote crowd verdicts as shipped until a vote path exists; when it does, that one clause is the only edit needed. Related copy not used, deliberately: the correspondence claims "we've spent nothing on paid marketing", which contradicts §8 (a ~$660 paid push around Apr 19 2026). Do not put that claim on any surface, investor-facing included.

- **Brand renamed to Debatable, domain unchanged** (2026-07-18). Aidan expanded the same-day app-only rename to the whole brand. Visible legacy naming was swept across titles, metadata, wordmarks, body copy, and email templates. Technical identifiers and structured-data aliases were initially preserved, then superseded by the sole-name sweep on 2026-07-25.

- **iOS app ships under the name "Debatable"** (2026-07-18). The App Store listing name and home-screen label are Debatable. The bundle identifier remains unchanged because it is the registered app identity, not public branding.
- **Reprice Individual $5→$10/year, Team $20→$50/year; free voice cut to 1/2** (2026-06-27). A unit-economics audit (real provider prices) showed the old $5/year Individual tier loses money on any engaged user once billing turns on, and voice (OpenAI Realtime) is ~80% of per-user variable cost. Aidan set the new prices himself: **Individual $10/year, Team $50/year** (BYOK $1/mo, Lifetime $14.99, Free $0 unchanged). Swept across all canonical surfaces (JSON-LD offers, pricing.html, in-app paywall/error strings, landing, compare pages, terms, story, report, geo.mjs INR → ₹838/₹4,190) in one commit, SW `v1284→v1285`. Coupled cost fix: **free voice hard-capped 1 anon / 2 signed-in** (was 3 / 8) — client gate (`voice-debate.html`), the real server enforcement (`realtime-session.mjs` `FREE_VOICE_LIFETIME_LIMIT 8→2`), and the gate copy. Voice is now the paid hook, not a giveaway. Note: prices are display-copy only until billing is switched on — checkout is still gated by `BETA_NO_CHARGE` (defaults true) in `create-checkout.mjs`, and `BETA_PRO_UNLOCK=true` in `index.html` still unlocks pro for everyone. Stripe→Firebase webhook path was audited and is wired end-to-end. The remaining provider-cost and premium-TTS follow-ups are tracked in the profitability and unit-economics strategy documents.
- **Lifetime repriced $14.99→$30, referral-code plan added to /pricing** (2026-07-02). Aidan asked for the Lifetime plan to move to **$30 once** and for pricing-page links to either land on the correct in-app plan or email `aidandavidhollinger@gmail.com`. `/pricing` CTAs now have real fallback hrefs: Free goes to `/app`, Individual/Lifetime go to `/app?upgrade=...`, BYOK and Team beta open mailto. Added a pricing-page referral-code plan: beta behavior is attribution first (`?ref=code` carried through sign-in and admin), post-beta rewards are tested only after paid conversion, and code owners include coaches, captains, organizers, ambassadors, and business champions. Swept current pricing surfaces and INR display for Lifetime `$30` / `₹2,514`.
- **Hero creed line removed from the landing** (2026-06-27). The rotating "voice-of-the-builder" creed under the hero ("AI as antagonist and tool. Not the Messiah, nor an arbitrary enemy.", "Built to challenge you. Not to flatter you.", etc.) was removed at Aidan's direct, repeated request. This **supersedes** the 2026-05-19 / 2026-05-23 entries that protected the creed as a permanent fixture ("the creed stays by design") — those described the stance at the time; the founder has since asked for the line gone. Both render elements were deleted from `app/landing.html`: the `<p class="hi-creed">` in the `.hero-illustrated` arm (the rotating one, driven by the `CREEDS` array + `swapCreed` rotator, which now no-ops on its `if (creed)` guard) and the static `<p class="hero-creed">` in the sign-in arm. The `CREEDS` array and the `.hi-creed` / `.hero-creed` CSS were left in place as inert dead code (no element references them); a later cleanup can strip them. Earlier the same day the same line had also been removed from the footer wordmark where a prior commit had relocated it. Net: no creed/tagline line renders under the hero or in the footer anymore. Do not re-add it without Aidan asking.
- **Earlier brand-name reframe, later superseded** (2026-06-08, corrected 2026-06-25). The product briefly used another name while keeping debateai.com as the owned production domain. The 2026-07-18 Debatable rename and the 2026-07-25 sole-name sweep supersede that public naming system.
- **Removed anonymous trial** (2026-04). The free-bypass was leaking usage and preventing a real paywall signal. Gmail-required is the only clean policy.
- **Removed magic-link auth** (2026-04). Firebase email-link wasn't enabled and "via Gmail" was the stated direction. Two paths = confusion; one is clearer. **REVERSED 2026-08-12, see the emailed-link entry.**
- **Reversed Gmail-required gate to advise-only** (2026-04-20). The full-screen pricing/sign-in gate locked out users whose browser couldn't load Firebase (iOS Safari ITP, Instagram/FB/TikTok in-app browsers, strict ad blockers). Too big a funnel leak to ignore. Now: anyone can enter with the 5-request anon cap; sign-in is advised via the topbar CTA + the post-2-generations nudge modal + feature-specific paywalls.
- **Tab shadows bumped to 30px vertical padding** (2026-04). `overflow-x:auto` on `.nav` clamps overflow-y to auto, clipping the red halo. 30px padding gives the shadow room to breathe.
- **Case Feedback moved from Other → Competitive** (2026-04). Post-round critique is a competitive-prep tool, not a support link.
- **Vocab Quiz added to Competitive** (2026-04). 52-term bank across debate flow / Policy / philosophy / elevated vocab. Multiple choice + flashcard mode, localStorage streak.
- **God mode trait preset** (2026-04). All strengths on, no weaknesses. `buildTraitPrompt` detects the combo and swaps to a dedicated world-champion prompt.
- **Audience pages use shared `/css/audience.css`** (2026-04). Level 1 distinctness is per-page `:root` override blocks for `--accent` and `--accent-glow`.
- **Per-format research allowance** (2026-04). `voice-guidelines.mjs` now has explicit research rules per format. Policy cards, PF citations, LD philosophy lit, parli no-evidence, Congress / MUN conversational cites. Prevents the AI from fabricating `Smith 2022` quotes in formats that don't use them.
- **APDA removed from CURRENT_TOPICS** (2026-04). Impromptu format; no rolling motion exists to hub. Users routed to Motions tab instead.
- **Counter sub-brand for the chrome extension** (2026-05-10). The chrome extension at `app/extension/` ships as **Counter: Oral Exam Trainer (by DebateAI)** — student-first, viva / oral-exam framed. The web app stays debater-first (per the 2026-04-20 commitment); the extension is a focused entry point for the "students" secondary audience listed in §2 (interview prep, admissions essays, thesis defense). This is NOT a contradiction with debate-first: same engine, different front door, sized for the highest-conversion entry context (highlight study material → defend out loud). India is the obvious primary market for this surface (80% of traffic, viva is universal in CBSE/ICSE board exams + IIT/NEET interviews + dissertation defenses). When future cleanup sweeps the surface for stale "DebateAI"-only branding, leave the extension's "Counter" labels alone.
- **Viva format added to FORMATS** (2026-05-10). New `viva` entry in `app/debate-ai.html` and `app/netlify/functions/lib/voice-guidelines.mjs`. Auto-selected when `?ext=1&mode=counter` is present. Two-speech structure (opening defense + cross-examination Q&A), no fabricated citations, plain academic register, examiner asks follow-ups under time pressure. This is the format the Counter extension defaults to.
- **Examiner persona ('Dr. Iyer')** (2026-05-10). New persona in `app/voice-debate.html` PERSONALITIES + `tts.mjs` voice maps. Indian-English measured academic tone via the OpenAI/Realtime `instructions` field; ElevenLabs falls back to its existing American voice (timbre stays American, register matches). Default persona for the Counter extension. Hindi-language vivas use the existing `aiLanguage='hi'` toggle, which already routes through the LLM and TTS pipelines.
- **Counter extension iframe switched to voice-debate.html** (2026-05-10). For oral-exam prep, the voice round (OpenAI Realtime) is the actual drill, not the typed flow. The extension side panel now loads `voice-debate.html?ext=1&mode=counter` instead of `debate-ai.html?ext=1&mode=counter`. The bridge useEffect in voice-debate.html prefills motion + auto-picks the Examiner persona + auto-picks `crossex` mode, landing the user one tap from "Connect."
- **Reverted the splash interstitial at /** (2026-05-15). Five days after adding `/splash.html` as the public face of the site at `/`, reverted it back to serving `/landing.html` at `/`. The splash had ~14 words of body content and zero outbound links (PREP/COMMUNITY removed the same day), so Google was indexing an empty page at the canonical URL `https://debateai.com/` while the 2,700-line marketing page with full SoftwareApplication + FAQPage structured data was buried at `/landing` with no inbound link equity. This is the root cause for the §8 "organic search is near zero" entry — not a content problem, a routing problem. The splash is still reachable at `/splash` for anyone who wants the voice-first intro; it just stops being the URL Google sees first. Paired with this: bumped the SoftwareApplication offers in the JSON-LD to the canonical pricing tiers (Free / BYOK $1 / Individual $5 / Lifetime $14.99 / Team $30), and added a `google-site-verification` meta placeholder so the user can verify ownership in GSC and finally see what queries the site ranks for.
- **Learning loop wired end-to-end** (2026-05-13). Closes §9 #4. Four surgical patches shipped: (1) `captureTurn` lives inside `callAnyAI` + `callClaude` in `app/index.html` so every typed AI turn (case, opp_attack, rebuttal, judge, debate-chat, casual, vision, resolution) lands in the `generations` Firestore collection with the full prompt → output triple, not just the case generator. (2) `voice-debate.html` `stop()` flushes the full turn-by-turn transcript to `/api/log-generation` with `kind:'voice_round'` so the WebRTC voice path stops being a blind spot. (3) New `app/netlify/functions/lib/exemplars.mjs` pulls 1–3 admin-weighted past rounds matching motion + format + side and prepends them as REFERENCE ROUNDS to `body.system`. Wired into all four brain functions (`claude.mjs`, `openai-chat.mjs`, `gemini.mjs`, `grok.mjs`) before `applyVoiceGuidelines`. Client (`callAnyAI` / `callClaude` in `index.html`) now sends `_motion` + `_side` in the body, stripped server-side after read. (4) New `scheduled-distill.mjs` runs nightly at 04:00 UTC over the previous 30 days of top-rated (rating ≥ 4 OR saved) generations per format, sends them to Claude Haiku with a strict "extract patterns" system prompt, writes the result to `learning_distillations/{format}`. `lib/distillations.mjs` reads that doc and appends a LEARNED PATTERNS block to `body.system` on every subsequent generation. Final system-prompt order: `[REFERENCE ROUNDS] + base system + [LEARNED PATTERNS] + [VOICE GUIDELINES]`. Voice rules come last so they win conflicts. Two 1-hour in-memory caches (admin uids, distillations) keep per-request Firestore reads near zero. Anon users can't log (server requires Firebase token), so this only captures signed-in usage — fine, that's the population we want.
- **Free tier bumped 5→15 and pricing surfaces softened** (2026-05-14). The 5-anon / 10-signed-in cap made the product feel paid-by-default; casual visitors hit the paywall before they understood the wedge. New caps: 15 anon, 30 signed-in (15 + 15). Per-pro-feature trial (Sneaky, Opp Attack, Competition Depth, Style Memory) bumped from 1 → 5 in `app/index.html` for the same reason. Pricing surfaces dialed back the same day: (a) `app/js/upgrade-cta.js` now only renders the floating "Upgrade" pill when the user has actually hit the cap, not as a persistent default-state ad on every page for every free user. (b) `app/js/usage-banner.js` hides the top-center usage banner for free users until 75%+ used (was 33%); paid users keep the graduated 50%/75%/90% severity tiers since budget awareness is genuinely useful for them. (c) `/pricing` removed from the `.landing-jumpnav` sticky in-page nav on `app/landing.html` — pricing info still reachable via the on-page FAQ "Pricing & practical stuff" block and the footer, just not surfaced as a top-level nav target. (d) Copy strings everywhere that said "5 free requests" / "all 10 free requests" / "Sign in for 5 more" got updated to either the new numbers or to ambient phrasing ("rounds left," "more on the house") so the per-action counter stops shouting the metering at the user. Updates to: `app/debate-ai.html` (ANON_LIMIT/SIGNED_IN_LIMIT consts + counter + modal copy), `app/index.html` (getAnonLimit, canUseProFeature, paywall modal, sign-up nudge), `app/learn.html` (ANON_BASE_LIMIT + banner copy), `app/high-school.html` (limit-hit copy), `app/counter.html` (hero pre-install + FAQ pricing line), `app/pricing.html` (Free-tier feature bullets), `app/report.html` (Free-tier row in the offers table), `app/landing.html` (JSON-LD FAQ answer + visible FAQ answer + pricing-card Free row), `app/extension/STORE_LISTING.md` (free-tier line). Server-side per-IP throttle in `app/netlify/functions/claude.mjs` (5/min, 40/hour, 150/day on `ANON_LAYERS`) is unchanged — that's an anti-abuse layer, not a paywall signal. Supersedes the 5-request principle in §4.
- **Beta-pricing framing, bento FAQ, villain-line removal** (2026-05-14, same day, third pass). Three more positioning moves:
  - **All tiers reframed as "future pricing — beta is free."** The product is officially in beta now; every pricing surface leads with "Currently free. The numbers below are what plans will look like after beta — share thoughts via feedback." Updates to: `app/pricing.html` (hero eyebrow + h1 + lede + A/B variant, new beta banner above the cards), `app/landing.html` FAQ "Is Debate AI free?" answer and the bigger "How much does it cost?" answer (both now lead with the beta state and link to the feedback form for pricing proposals), `app/debate-ai.html` paywall modal (eyebrow "Beta cap reached" + body explains beta + button now routes to the feedback Google Form, not /pricing), `app/js/upgrade-cta.js` (title "Help shape future pricing"; sub "Beta is free. Pricing proposals welcome."; CTA renamed "Feedback" + routed to the feedback form instead of /pricing). soul.md §4 + §5 + §7 updated: "Free during beta" lifted off the banned-phrases list since the product is now genuinely in beta with explicit future-pricing-for-reference framing. The literal "go to feedback for better pricing proposals" phrasing the user asked for is reused verbatim across surfaces so the message is consistent.
  - **Landing FAQ converted to a bento layout.** Replaced the uniform 3-column grid with a 6-column `grid-auto-flow: dense` bento where each tile is sized by editorial weight: `.faq-item--big` (3col × 2row) for wedge-defining questions (What is Debate AI / How is this different / How much does it cost / Is using this to prep cheating), `.faq-item--wide` (3col × 1row) for substantive secondaries, `.faq-item--circle` (2col × 1row, `aspect-ratio:1/1`, `border-radius:50%`) for short punchy questions ("Which debate formats?" / "Get started in 60 seconds" / "Bring your own API key?" / "Share cases with my team?" / "Who's behind this?"), and the rest as default 1×1 tiles. Open circles transition out of the disc shape into a normal rounded card so the answer panel fits; on mobile (<680px) all tiles collapse to a single column and aspect ratios are killed (a circle in a 1-col stack is awful). The `#faq > .wrap` max-width bumped 1240 → 1480 to give the bento room to breathe. Big-tile copy gets a larger non-uppercase question font so the size hierarchy reads visually, not just structurally.
  - **Villain lines removed sitewide.** The hero "The chatbot agrees with you. We don't." beat on `app/landing.html` was cut along with its CSS rule. The whole `.why-different` section (60+ lines: "Why Debate AI, not ChatGPT — Grounded in real rounds. Not just internet text. ChatGPT doesn't have any of that. It also agrees with you. This doesn't.") got deleted. The landing FAQ "How is this different from just using ChatGPT?" was renamed to "How is this different from a general-purpose AI?" and rewritten to describe what the product does rather than what ChatGPT allegedly fails at. The pricing.html "Does it actually make me better, or is it just another ChatGPT?" FAQ question and "ChatGPT will generate you a persuasive blob" answer got rewritten the same way. The counter.html "Is this just another AI chatbot in a side panel?" question was rewritten as "How is this different from chatting with an AI tutor?" without the villain framing. The topics/index.html "Most 'AI debate' tools give you a generic chatbot" line and the extension/STORE_LISTING.md "Not a chatbot pretending to be a tutor" line both lost the villain phrasing. The index.html "Debate-trained, not generic AI" pill lost its "not generic AI" half. Decision: positive product framing only — sneering at the out-group reads as insecure and contradicts the "varsity debater on the circuit, not philosophy seminar" register the AI itself is supposed to use. Banned-phrase list in soul.md §5 updated.
- **Individual repriced $5/mo → $5/year, Voice "Pro" rebranded to "New", FAQ regridded** (2026-05-14, same day, second pass). Three coupled product-positioning moves:
  - **Individual: $5/mo → $5/year.** The previous $5/mo target tier was being mentally compared to Netflix ($15-22/mo) and Spotify ($11/mo) — same shape (monthly SaaS), so the user's frame was "is this worth a recurring monthly bill?" At $5/year the frame switches to "is this worth a single tournament entry fee?" which is a trivially-yes answer for anyone who debates. Annual billing also kills churn-rate volatility. Updated in: `app/landing.html` (JSON-LD offer description + visible FAQ answer + pricing-rows card), `app/pricing.html` (JSON-LD + title + meta og/twitter + lede + Individual card cadence/button/note + FAQ entry on under-50-requests + Lifetime + Team subtitles), `app/debate-ai.html` (JSON-LD + paywall-modal "signed-in quota used" body), `app/index.html` (callAnyAI + callClaude error strings, pricing-grid in both signed-out and signed-in states, BYOK-cross-provider error, paywall-modal `/month` → `/year` cadence in localizedPrice block), `app/counter.html` (FAQ pricing line), `app/report.html` (header + Individual row + closing margin paragraph), `app/india.html` (rupee/USD mix), `app/us.html` (USD mix), `app/extension/STORE_LISTING.md` (free-tier line), `app/js/upgrade-cta.js` (pill title), `app/netlify/functions/lib/auth.mjs` (paid-feature error message). JSON-LD `price` field kept as "5" (still $5) — only the cadence/recurrence framing changed. BYOK ($1/mo = $12/yr) is now nominally more expensive than Individual ($5/yr), but BYOK serves a different niche: power users who'd rather route everything through their own Anthropic key for cost transparency / privacy / having the receipts. Lifetime ($14.99 once) is now ~3 years of Individual — still a meaningful upsell for users who want to forget about renewing.
  - **Voice rebranded: "Pro" tag → "New" tag.** On `app/landing.html` the hero pill rail and hero CTA rail both labeled Voice with a gold-on-amber `Pro` tag (CSS classes `hero-pill-pro` / `hero-cta-rail-pro` / `hero-pill-tag` / `hero-cta-rail-tag`). That framing put Voice mentally in the "paid feature locked behind a tier" bucket. Renamed CSS classes to `-new` and recolored from gold/amber to brand red (the same red as the primary CTA) so the badge reads as a fresh-feature highlight, not a paywall flag. Paired with the rename, the voice gate in `app/voice-debate.html` bumped its free caps (anon 1 → 3, signed-in 3 → 8) so the label isn't lying — Voice is now genuinely more accessible, not just relabeled. Updated copy on the upgrade gate ("Upgrade to Pro for unlimited voice" → "Keep going with a plan") and the sign-in gate ("Sign in for 3 more rounds" → "Sign in for 8 more voice rounds"). The TTS HD voice provider selector inside `app/debate-ai.html` (ElevenLabs / Inworld vs free OpenAI gpt-4o-mini-tts) keeps its `Pro` tag — that one is a genuine paid premium-voice upsell, not the Voice-round feature itself.
  - **Landing FAQ regridded into 3 columns.** `app/landing.html` `#faq` wrap max-width bumped 780 → 1240 (override on the `!important` rule at line 2636). The inner container converted from a vertical stack to `.faq-grid` with `grid-template-columns:repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr))`; group headings (`.faq-group-head`) span the full row via `grid-column:1 / -1`. Drops to 2 columns under 1024px and 1 column under 680px so mobile reading order stays linear. Visual goal: FAQ now uses the available page width instead of a tall narrow rail nobody scrolled to the bottom of.

- **Team $30/mo → $20/year** (2026-05-14, fifth pass). Extending the annual-pricing framing to Team. At $20/year for 50 seats, the math is ~$0.40/seat/year — cheaper-than-anything for a whole debate club. Updated in: `app/landing.html` (JSON-LD + pricing-rows Team card), `app/pricing.html` (JSON-LD + Team `<article>` card: $30/mo → $20/year, 2,000 → 1,500 requests/mo, 30 → 50 seats, fine print), `app/debate-ai.html` (JSON-LD), `app/index.html` (both pricing-grid blocks, the upgrade-modal `localizedPrice(30→20)` + Team card `/month` → `/year` + "1,500 requests · 50 members" → "1,500 requests/mo · 50 seats", the team-card price string in the in-app paywall row), `app/us.html` (footer pricing), `app/extension/STORE_LISTING.md`, `app/report.html` (Team row + the "ARPU multiplier" line referencing the $30 tier), `app/netlify/functions/lib/geo.mjs` RAZORPAY_INR_PRICES (Individual ₹399/mo → ₹419/year, Lifetime ₹1,499 → ₹1,249, Team ₹2,400/mo → ₹1,675/year), `soul.md` §7 + `AGENTS.md`'s "Pricing is locked" line + the "ask before doing" pricing-tier line. JSON-LD `price` field kept as "20" (still $20) — only the cadence/recurrence framing changed.

- **Two new brains, same day: DeepSeek + Open Lab** (2026-05-15, sixth pass). The stack went 4 brains → 6 brains via two parallel commits that arrived hours apart. First commit (576cb71) added `deepseek` — direct DeepSeek API proxy at `app/netlify/functions/deepseek.mjs` (mirrored to root), pinned to `deepseek-chat`, color `#4D6BFE`, paired with per-task brain routing (BRAIN_TASKS map: case gen → Claude, judging → DeepSeek, rebuttals → Grok, current-events → Gemini), multi-brain panel judging that fans the ballot to DeepSeek + Claude + GPT in parallel with a consensus banner, /exhibition AI-vs-AI orchestrator, and the Sparring Roster v0.1. Second commit (this entry's branch) added `openlab` — OpenRouter-backed proxy at `app/netlify/functions/openlab.mjs` (mirrored to root), default model `nousresearch/hermes-4-405b`, color `#a78bfa`, backend allow-list also covers Hermes 3 (fallback), Mistral Large 2, Qwen3-235B, Llama 4 Maverick/Scout — env override via `OPENLAB_MODEL` / `OPENLAB_ALLOWED_MODELS`. Both are Pro-tier only (`requirePaidPlan`) with identical applyExemplars / applyVoiceGuidelines / applyDistillations plumbing so the learning loop captures their turns the same way as the original four. Both also got their own env var: DeepSeek wants `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY`, Open Lab wants `OPENROUTER_API_KEY`. Pitch separation: DeepSeek is the cheap reasoner — sharp, technical, default for AI-judged ballots so the verdict has no house-style preference. Open Lab is the human-voice pool — Nous Hermes 4 was picked specifically for character-rich prose and low refusal rates ("THBT the means justify the ends" style motions where the big-lab brains get hedge-y). Realtime council in `realtime-session.mjs` deliberately stays at the original four — adding two more parallel calls adds noticeable latency to session start for marginal prep diversity. "4 brains" → "6 brains" sweep across: `app/index.html` (CASE_BRAINS, three paywall/error strings, the "GPT/Gemini/Grok still count" notice now reads "GPT, Gemini, Grok, DeepSeek, and Open Lab still count"), `app/debate-ai.html` (BRAINS, typed-mode preamble, upgrade-popover, "Upgrade for all 6 brains" link, JSON-LD), `app/landing.html` (proof-item card → "6 brains / Claude · GPT · Gemini · Grok · DeepSeek · Open Lab", pricing-rows Individual blurb, JSON-LD, sign-in error fallback), `app/voice-debate.html` (typed-mode-vs-voice explainer), `app/pricing.html` (Individual / Lifetime / Team feature lists), `app/counter.html` (FAQ "Who built this"), `app/report.html` (Individual row), `app/extension/STORE_LISTING.md` (positioning paragraph), `AGENTS.md` ("ask before doing" line), `soul.md` §3 wedge #4 + §7 pricing-table.

- **Devil's Advocate brand sunset** (2026-05-18). The mirror domain `debatethedevil.com` and the dead Netlify deploy URL `https://devilsadvocate1.netlify.app` were removed from every surface that could be cleaned without breaking real product features. Sweep covered: (a) the `contact@devilsadvocateteam.com` team email replaced sitewide with `feedback@debateai.com` (16 surfaces — terms, privacy, schools, pricing, all footers, mailto subjects). (b) CORS allowlists in all 13 Netlify brain/api functions (claude/openai-chat/gemini/grok/deepseek/openlab/tts/argument-lint/docs-agent/billing-portal/realtime-session/lib-response, plus the mirrored root copies for deepseek + openlab) dropped both `debatethedevil.com` and `devilsadvocate1.netlify.app` entries — anyone still hitting the old mirror from a browser now gets a CORS denial. (c) `billing-portal.mjs` final-fallback URL `https://debatethedevil.com` → `https://debateai.com`. (d) `app/extension/manifest.json` host_permission for `https://debatethedevil.com/*` removed (the chrome extension's side panel stops loading on the mirror; debateai.com is the only supported origin). (g) `soul.md` §8 "Live at debatethedevil.com" → "Live at debateai.com" and AGENTS.md header line trimmed. 23 files, -41 net stale lines. What stays: the `/devils-advocate` route is a legitimate product feature (the single-AI sparring mode, distinct from the multi-format trainer at `/app`), so its filename and admin-report label stay. If the mirror domain `debatethedevil.com` is later retired entirely at the DNS level, no further code action is needed; the codebase no longer references it. **2026-06-14 reversal (email only):** Aidan asked to point contact email back to `contact@devilsadvocateteam.com`, so the sitewide sweep was undone for the address — every user-facing `mailto:` link and the JSON-LD contact `email` field across the static HTML pages + `signup-nudge`/loaders now read `contact@devilsadvocateteam.com` again (the Counter chrome extension's `sidepanel.js` was left on the old address since it ships through the store separately). The domain/CORS/mirror cleanup from this entry STAYS; only the contact inbox flipped back. Don't re-sweep the email to `feedback@debateai.com`.

- **Multilingual end-to-end wiring** (2026-05-18). Audit caught that the i18n infrastructure shipped half-built: landing.html had a working `<select>` picker (Google Translate + `debateos-locale` localStorage); debate-ai.html had its own 14-language `aiLanguage` picker that injected a directive into `buildPrompt()`; **but** index.html (the `/app` surface) had `SUPPORTED_LOCALES`, the I18nContext, `loadLocale()`, and `t()` lookups all working — yet no picker in the topbar (lost in a refactor) and no language directive on any brain call, so signed-in `/app` users could land in Hindi UI but the case generator still replied in English. voice-debate.html had aspirational comments referencing `aiLanguage='hi'` but the field was never sent or read, and the realtime transcription was hard-pinned to `language:'en'` which mis-transcribes non-English audio. Five surgical patches: (1) `app/index.html` adds a `buildLanguageBlock()` helper that reads `debateos-locale` and emits a non-negotiable LANGUAGE directive at the head of the system prompt; wired into both `callAnyAI` and `callClaude` so every typed-mode AI surface (case gen, battle, judge, vision, casual, philosopher) now respects the picked locale. (2) `app/index.html` topbar gets a language-picker pill that overlays an invisible `<select>` on a visible flag-and-code pill matching the theme/style/Donate pill row — same UI pattern the original commit shipped before the refactor dropped it. (3) `app/voice-debate.html` reads `debateos-ai-lang || debateos-locale` and posts it as `aiLanguage` in the `/api/realtime-session` mint body, also mirrors it onto `window.__aiLanguage` so the dc.onopen `session.update` (separate closure) can pin `transcription.language` to the real user language instead of hardcoded `'en'`. (4) `app/netlify/functions/realtime-session.mjs` reads `body.aiLanguage`, maps it through a `REALTIME_LANG_NAMES` allow-list (14 languages mirroring debate-ai.html's `LANGUAGES`), and prepends a `LANGUAGE: Speak the entire round in {name}` directive at the top of the instructions stack so the spoken AI honors the user's language pick. (5) `app/netlify/functions/tts.mjs` Cartesia path had a hardcoded `language: 'en'` (sonic-2 requires the field but accepts any language code) — now threaded through from a new `body.language` field (BCP-47 short codes, regex-validated, default `'en'`). `app/debate-ai.html` TTS call sites (fetchTTS + fetchTTSBlob) now post `language` from `debateos-ai-lang || debateos-locale`. ElevenLabs `eleven_turbo_v2_5` and OpenAI `gpt-4o-mini-tts` already auto-detect from input text so the field is harmless on those paths and load-bearing only on Cartesia. Result: a user picks Hindi on landing → reaches `/app` → UI strings hydrate from `/i18n/hi.json` → AI brain calls (typed + voice) all reply in Hindi → TTS synthesizes Hindi audio across every provider. Pre-existing translation-coverage gap (many hardcoded React-tree strings still in English on `/app`) is a separate concern from this infrastructure wiring; that's a `t()`-call sweep, not a multilingual-system fix.

- **Netlify credit-burn audit: scheduled trims + soft-cap rollback** (2026-05-18, two passes). The user opened a Netlify dashboard showing credits draining unexpectedly fast; analysis flagged 63 functions with the dominant burn sources being the keep-alive cron, the 5-min kickoff-reminder cron, the nightly distillation/fingerprint Haiku passes, and a generous anon-IP day cap that bots could rotate against. **First pass (invocation + cron trims):** (a) Deleted `app/netlify/functions/scheduled-keepalive.mjs` entirely. Was already dropped 5min → 15min on 2026-05-16 and still bleeding (4 runs/hr × 5 invocations ≈ 14.4K/month). The 2-5s cold-start penalty on the first off-peak user is the trade. Brain functions still work without it; the file's own comment documented deletion as the disable path. (b) `scheduled-kickoff-reminder.mjs` 5-min cron → 15-min cron; window widened 5-15 min → 5-25 min so coverage stays gapless and the `reminderSent` flag still dedupes. Saves ~5.7K invocations/month. Reminder now fires 5-25 min before kickoff (median ~15) instead of 5-15 (median ~10). (c) `claude.mjs` `ANON_LAYERS` tightened: hour 40 → 25, day 150 → 60. Same-day second pass dropped further to hour 15, day 30 once the user-facing caps came down. The user-facing soft cap is the friendly paywall; the IP layers exist only to throttle bot abuse rotating on a single IP. (d) `scheduled-user-fingerprint.mjs` nightly cap 60 → 30 users, freshness 7 → 14 days. Style fingerprints describe an argumentative identity and don't drift weekly. Halves nightly Haiku spend (~$0.18 → ~$0.09). Env overrides `FINGERPRINT_MAX_USERS`/`FINGERPRINT_FRESH_DAYS` remain. **Second pass (soft-cap rollback):** the 2026-05-14 bump from 5/10 → 15/30 was a conversion-funnel move, but with the product still in beta nothing converts anyway and the wider funnel doubled drain on real users PLUS doubled bot abuse on the same lever. Reverted to 5 anon / 10 signed-in across `app/debate-ai.html` (ANON_LIMIT + SIGNED_IN_LIMIT consts + JSON-LD + quota-modal copy + counter-banner copy + paywall comment), `app/index.html` (ANON_BASE_LIMIT + getAnonLimit + "sign in for 5 more" copy), `app/learn.html` (ANON_BASE_LIMIT), `app/counter.html` (FAQ), `app/pricing.html` (JSON-LD + cancellation FAQ), `app/report.html` (Free row), `app/landing.html` (JSON-LD FAQ answer + visible pricing-rows Free card), `app/extension/STORE_LISTING.md` (free-tier line). Per-pro-feature trial (Sneaky / Opp Attack / Competition Depth / Style Memory) `canUseProFeature` in `app/index.html` reverted 5 → 1 for the same reason — each premium-feature try is a pro-brain call on an unconverted user. Pricing-surface softening from 2026-05-14 (upgrade-cta.js render conditions, usage-banner 75% threshold, jumpnav Pricing link removal) is **left in place** — the issue was the cap size, not how aggressive the pricing surfaces were. soul.md §4 + §7 rewritten; this entry supersedes the 2026-05-14 "Free tier bumped 5→15" entry above. AGENTS.md "Free tier" line + the "ask before doing" pricing-cap reference updated to match.

Pulsing white dot inside still carries the "live, talk right now" signal. `app/high-school.html` "Why Parliamentary?" eyebrow, pricing-card border, upgrade-banner gradient + border, "Upgrade →" link all flipped amber → red. Kept as functional amber/gold (not touched): rate-limit warn banners, error toasts, low-rating star ratings, achievement tier-legendary gold (semantic tier indicator), `bar-warn` admin row, "AI Fallback" spar.html status pill, voice-debate persona avatar tints (per-persona color identity), profile.html lifetime-tier gold chrome (premium tier indicator), format-color associations in `profile.html` (BP=gold, Asian Parli=orange are category colors, not decoration). Brand discipline: amber/gold now reserved for state semantics (warn, achievement, tier); red is the brand-action color across all CTAs.

- **Per-format brain training: 4 parallel multi-hour research agents → 11-commit voice-guidelines synthesis** (2026-05-19, big late-day pass). Closes the long-standing wedge claim in §3 — "Format accuracy. Policy gets tagged-card delivery. LD gets value/criterion. BP gets extensions + whip structure. APDA is impromptu with no fake citations." Until today that was true at the *minimum competence* layer but not at the *2026-current-meta* layer; every generic LLM had the same training data and missed the recent changes (Sofia 2026 WUDC manual, India's WSDC 2025 win, NDT 2026 NEG dominance, the real WPM correction 260-320 not 350-450, the Arctic topic meta, the Sofia POI mandate). **Architecture:** four background `general-purpose` research agents spent 2-3 hours each researching format clusters (Policy + LD; PF + Congress + MUN; BP + Worlds + APDA; Asian Parli + WSDC + India circuit). Each returned a structured markdown findings doc with proposed `FORMAT_VOICES` patches + new `TOPIC_PRIMERS` + real-author libraries + tournament texture + failure modes — all sourced to verified URLs (worlddebating.com, calicotab tabs, openCaselist wiki, NSDA topic announcements, Tabroom paradigms, APDA results database, ISDS, IIMUN). **Synthesized into voice-guidelines.mjs across 11 commits:** (1) BP/WUDC/APDA depth pass — Sofia 2026 manual changes (POIs MANDATORY now, generative-AI banned in 15-min prep, whip flexibility for reactive analysis, closed-round confidentiality, ranked-pairs voting), full Imperial speaker-scale bands (50-100 with explicit decent/strong/exceptional targets), motion-shape playbook (THBT/THW/THR/THS/THO/as-actor/preference), verified 2024-26 champions (Oxford Hanoi 2024, Dartmouth Panama 2025, Sydney Sofia 2026 — CRITICAL correction since most LLM priors have WUDC 2026 as Belgrade not Sofia; Belgrade was the 2022 virtual edition), tight-call full taxonomy + elite speech-position playbook for APDA, recent Nationals winners + TOTY + SOTY through 2025-26. (2) Asian Parli + WSDC depth pass + **two new FORMAT_VOICES keys** — `india_school` and `india_college`, directly targeting the 80% India traffic. india_school covers CBSE/ICSE/IB schools running Asian Parli + WSDC + Frank Anthony Memorial, tier-1 schools (DPS RKP, Vasant Valley, Shri Ram Moulsari, Emerald Heights Indore, Pathways, Cathedral Mumbai, La Martiniere), tournament list (Frank Anthony, India Today Cup, DPS RKP Outspoken, IDL, ISDC, MSDL), Indian-English register guide (KEEP invariant "isn't it" tags, syllable-timed prosody, formal salutations, "kindly note" / "do the needful" / "I would humbly submit" — don't over-correct to flat American TTS), India-context example library covering Supreme Court cases (Navtej / Puttaswamy / Sabarimala / NJC / Article 370 / CAA-NRC / Farm Laws / Agnipath / NEP 2020), South Asia (Bangladesh Hasina ouster 2024, Sri Lanka bankruptcy, Maldives Muizzu pivot, Pakistan Imran Khan / military), region-specific religion frames, non-philosophy-name-drop rule (reach for Ambedkar / Gandhi / Tagore / Sen / Nehru first, not Kant / Rawls / Mill). Hindi-language register section with core debate vocab (मुद्दा / पक्ष / विपक्ष / तर्क / सबूत), formal Hindi parliamentary openings, Lok Sabha-derived rhetorical cadence patterns, the unique-to-Hindi-vaad-vivaad interjector role, periodic SOV Hindi grammar (don't translate-from-English). india_college covers NLSIU / IIT Bombay / SRCC / NMIMS / BITS / NLU Delhi / RVCE / Ramjas / St. Stephen's circuit, both Asian Parli and BP in use, ABP as pipeline tournament. 14 India aliases added to `forFormat` synonym map ("india today cup," "frank anthony," "isdc," "outspoken," "nls debate," "abp," "iit bombay iv," "srcc gambit," "gambit" all route correctly). (3) PF + Congress + MUN depth pass — full PF speech order + times, 1st-vs-2nd speaker asymmetry, issue summary collapse, full 10 weighing mechanisms (not just 4: scope / severity / reversibility / magnitude / probability / timeframe / urgency / prerequisite / link-in / short-circuit), 4-point judge spectrum (lay / flay / flow / tech). Congress: seven speech positions as different jobs, precedence/recency PO logic, parliamentary motion catalog, 2026 docket categorized. MUN: committee types as basically different formats (GA / Crisis / Specialized / JCC), position paper grading specs (BMUN / HMUN formats), 3-ring bloc model, verified UN resolution library (UNSC 1325/2231/1373/1540/1701/2118, UNGA 2758, UN Charter Articles 2(4)/2(7)/51/Chapter VI vs VII, 1951 Refugee Convention, ICCPR/ICESCR, 1968 NPT), India circuit (IIMUN 150K+ schools, HMUN India, DAIMUN). (4) Policy + LD depth pass — actual speaking speed correction (260-320 wpm per The 3NR 2021 study, not the 350-450 software-chart myth), read-a-card SHAPE (tag → cite → body, three beats with the qualifier matters MORE in Policy than any other format), 2025-26 Arctic meta in depth (four AFF tracks Military/Cooperation/Indigenous-Env/Energy-Mining, icebreaker load-bearing factoid US 2 vs Russia 40+, ICE Pact + Trump Greenland uniqueness anchors, K-aff scene Decolonial Fugitivity/Red Deal/Cartography-K, T-shells specific to Arctic), 2025-26 Labor meta (NDT/CEDA: 37% AFF win rate in elims, K dominance, top AFFs Federal Workers / Bankruptcy / Sectoral / Gig / Moon-workers), K-aff vs T-FW pivot, personal narrative K-affs (Binghamton 2025 NDT wedge). LD: trad vs circuit audience-split rule (default trad unless circuit signal), full 11-framework bank with real anchors (Kantian Korsgaard, Util Parfit, Scanlonian, Rawlsian, Virtue Aristotle/MacIntyre, Hobbesian, Pettit, Galtung Structural Violence, Pummer Moderate Deontology, Bostrom/Ord Existential Risk, Mackie Moral Skepticism), circuit theory mechanics, 2025-26 full LD topic cycle. (5) 12 new ELITE MOVE additions to STRATEGY block (FOUR-WALL BREAK / TAX FRAME / META-COLLAPSE / ASYMMETRIC CONCESSION / UNOBVIOUS COUNTERFACTUAL / WEDGE / ENGAGEMENT TEST / ROLE INVERSION / PRINCIPLED-PRACTICAL SPLIT / CRYSTALLIZATION CALLBACK / BACKHANDED EXTENSION / BURDEN-INVERTING POI) with worked examples drawn from real recent finals. (6) **Server-side topic auto-classifier infrastructure** at `app/netlify/functions/lib/voice-guidelines.mjs` — `inferTopicFromText` with regex word-boundary + plural/gerund tolerance + body-extraction across Claude/OpenAI/Gemini shapes, conservative `MIN_TOPIC_SCORE = 4` threshold (two strong matches or one strong + one medium, or four mediums). Critical infrastructure: the entire `TOPIC_PRIMERS` system had shipped weeks ago with `FINANCE_PRIMER` defined, but the client never passed `_voiceTopic` so primers sat dormant. This auto-classifier closes the gap; primers now fire automatically when motion keywords match, no client work required. Each new primer is a two-place edit (primer const + `TOPIC_KEYWORDS` entry). (7-11) **19 TOPIC_PRIMERS total** added across the synthesis (1 pre-existing FINANCE + 5 cross-cutting strategy: ARCTIC / COLLECTIVE_BARGAINING / HEGEMONY / CAPITALISM_K / CRITICAL_PHIL_K; 6 PF monthly cycle: pf_uk_eu / pf_encryption / pf_china_extraction / pf_sports_betting / pf_zoning / pf_war_powers; 3 LD topic cycle: plea_bargaining / rewilding / nuclear_weapons; 4 cross-cutting domain: ai_governance / climate / criminal_justice / immigration). Each primer is ~30-50 lines of grounded domain context — factual anchors, real authors with works, real organizations, framework pairings, contention archetypes, weighing tilts. Test harness at `scripts/test-topic-classifier.mjs`, 53 / 0 fail. (12) JUDGE_RULES in `app/live-round.html` augmented across all 9 existing format keys + **two new keys** (wsdc + mun) with the same depth additions applied from the judging side — Sofia 2026 manual for BP / Worlds, full WUDC speaker scale bands, 2024-26 verified champions as benchmarks, WSDC India 2025 win + conferral adjudication + per-country team styles, Asian Parli definitional-debate adjudication, LD trad-vs-circuit + theory-shell adjudication, PF 1st-vs-2nd asymmetry penalties, Policy WPM-myth correction + K-aff vs T-FW evaluation framework + Arctic-topic-specific T-shells, Congress seven-speech-positions + PO ranking, full MUN committee-type evaluation framework. **Net architectural impact:** voice block grew from ~30K → ~60K chars across `FULL` system prompt. Every brain endpoint (Claude / GPT / Gemini / Grok / DeepSeek / Open Lab) now prepends FULL + format block + topic primer (auto-classified) + exemplars + distillations + user fingerprint before the LLM call. This is what "uniquely better trained than a normal LLM at debate" looks like at the prompt layer. Critical recent-meta corrections embedded that no other LLM has in its priors: WUDC 2026 = Sofia not Belgrade; Sofia 2026 manual changes (POI mandatory now); India won WSDC 2025 9-0 vs Australia; real WPM = 260-320; Trump 2025 Greenland gambit + October 2025 White House Arctic Security Cutters EO + November 2025 ICE Pact ministerial as Arctic-topic uniqueness anchors; NDT 2026 = Emory GS won, K dominance, 37% AFF win rate in elims; APDA 2026 Nationals = William & Mary surprise win, Sophie Rukin top-speaker as freshman. Commits `4ebc1df` → `bad6510`. SW `v455 → v479`. The brain-training thread in HQ ([[DebateAI — HQ]]) flips from "scoping" to "shipped at prompt layer + Aidan still needs to rate generations at /admin/rate to compound through the nightly distill loop."

- **Spar waitlist + private DM marketplace** (2026-05-20). First async community surface (progresses open question §9 #5). The /spar page was synchronous-only: you queued, waited 60s, and either matched a random human or fell through to the AI. A slow queue dead-ended you. New left rail on `app/spar.html` ("Open invites") is a one-tap **waitlist signup**: signed-in users hit "Join the waitlist" (note OPTIONAL — joining alone marks you available; a note just adds color), which writes a doc to the new `waitlist_posts` Firestore collection. Note-less signups render "Open to a round. DM to organize." Once joined, the action slot flips to a green "You're on the waitlist" confirmation + a Leave button (membership derived from the live posts feed via `myWaitlistPost()`, so no duplicate posts). Anyone signed in can browse the list and DM a waitlister privately to organize a debate on their own time. (Note: this shipped first as "post an invite" with a *required* note, then was reframed same-day to the lighter one-tap signup — the marketplace data model is identical, only the join UX got lighter.) DMs live in `dm_threads/{threadId}/messages` with `threadId = sorted([uidA,uidB]).join('_')`; thread docs carry participants + participantInfo + lastMessage + an unread counter map. Inbox button with unread badge (mirrored onto the mobile FAB), real-time `onSnapshot` on both the posts feed and the thread. Posts have a 24h client-side TTL filter (kept in Firestore until the author deletes). Read-only teaser on the signed-out gate (social proof + sign-in nudge); posting + DMing require Google sign-in (same anti-bot posture as the matchmaker). Mobile: rail becomes a slide-in drawer behind a floating FAB. Required Firestore rules (waitlist_posts + dm_threads + messages subcollection) and the composite index (`dm_threads`: participants ARRAY_CONTAINS + lastMessageAt DESC) are documented inline in spar.html above `WL_POST_TTL_MS`; both are console-only actions the rules don't ship from the repo. Same commit: the /spar gate + searching **orb** got a visual pass — was a flat red gradient blob, now a layered sphere (specular-highlight + shadow gradient for 3D, two staggered expanding ring pulses, a conic radar sweep, three orbiting satellite dots at different radii/speeds) with the live world-globe.js canvas pins still drawing on top at z-index 3. The orb reads as a rich animated sphere even if world-globe.js fails to load.

- **Voice-round rating widget moved to the ballot page** (2026-05-24). Closes the §9 #4 open follow-up on session-end rating prompts. The 1-5 star + boring + notes widget had been wired on `app/voice-debate.html` since the learning-loop pass (2026-05-13), but it was dead code in the happy path: `generateRFD` writes a payload to `sessionStorage['debateos-voice-rfd']` and navigates the user to `/voice-rfd` via `window.location.href = '/voice-rfd'`, which destroys the React state holding `lastVoiceRoundId`. `/voice-rfd` had no rating UI of its own. Net effect: every voice round that reached the ballot generated zero labels for the nightly `scheduled-distill.mjs` pass, so the voice path was running the distill loop blind — the "PATTERNS THAT WORK" block compounded only from the typed-mode generations Aidan manually rated at `/admin/rate`. Two surgical patches: (a) `app/voice-debate.html` `generateRFD` payload now carries `generationId: lastVoiceRoundId || null` and `format: mode || ''`, so the destination page knows which Firestore doc to write the rate signal onto. (b) `app/voice-rfd.html` gets a new `.rate-card` section between the ballot blocks and the CTA row, gated on `payload.generationId` AND `firebase.auth().currentUser`. Hidden by default; the auth-check IIFE waits up to 2.5s for `onAuthStateChanged` to settle before giving up (anon visitors never see it). Stars POST `action:'signal', signal:'rate', value:n` to `/api/log-generation` immediately; the boring toggle + notes textarea re-fire the rate signal with the merged meta, matching the existing widget's shape so `log-generation.mjs` needs zero changes. Notes textarea auto-surfaces when rating ≤ 2 OR boring is flagged — debounced 1.2s, plus a blur-flush belt-and-suspenders. Eyebrow swaps to "Rated N/5 — thanks" on first click so the user sees they're not shouting into a void. Amber stars + red boring toggle = state semantics, not chrome (per the 2026-05-19 amber-reserved-for-state rule). GA4 `voice_rfd_rate` event ships alongside each POST for funnel visibility. Commit `7fdefb6`. Net architectural impact: the voice path is no longer the silent partner in the distill loop. Every signed-in voice round that reaches the ballot now has a one-tap path to becoming labeled training data. The `applyDistillations` voice block per format gets stronger with each rated round, not just with each typed round Aidan rates by hand.

- **Distillation freshness on /admin** (2026-05-24, third pass). Closes the last open follow-up under §9 #4 (Learning loop). The nightly `scheduled-distill.mjs` cron writes `learning_distillations/{slug}` docs at 04:00 UTC, but until now the only signal it actually ran was digging through Netlify function logs — easy to miss a regression for weeks. New `app/netlify/functions/admin-distillations.mjs` (+ mirror) reads all 9 format docs in parallel (cheap: 9 Firestore reads/call), returns per-format age + exampleCount + model + 200-char preview + a stale flag (>36h, leaves a 12h grace window for one missed cron before the alarm rings), sorted stale-first within stale and oldest-first within fresh so a regression sorts to the top. Cached 5min via `admin-cache` so dashboard refreshes don't re-read. New "Learning loop · nightly distill" card on `/admin` renders a table with red pills for stale or missing formats, quiet greys for fresh ones, and a meta header showing "N stale · M/9 present" for at-a-glance health. Same admin-only auth pattern as the other admin-* endpoints. Commit `d7f989d`. Net: the nightly heartbeat is now visible on the dashboard you already check daily, not hidden in a log surface you only open after a regression.

- **Light-theme dashboard contrast rebuild** (2026-05-20). The Saved-tab dashboard cards in `app/debate-ai.html` (Saved cases / Round history / How I think as a debater / Practice drill / Record vs. AI) were near-invisible on light theme. Root cause: `[data-lighting="light"]` set `--c-surface: rgba(0,0,0,.025)` — a 2.5% black tint on the `#f5f5f0` cream bg, i.e. invisible cards — plus `--c-dim` at .28 alpha for dates (~2:1 contrast) and hardcoded `rgba(255,255,255,.02)` inner-row backgrounds in the JSX (white-on-cream, also invisible). The light-theme surface vars were authored as dark-on-cream tints copied from the dark theme's white-on-black logic, which only works at much higher alphas. Fix flips surface direction on light theme: surfaces are now BRIGHTER than the bg (solid `#ffffff` / `#fbfaf5`) with real borders + box-shadow elevation, dim/subtle/faint text alphas bumped to clear WCAG AA, and the hardcoded row tints swapped to `var(--c-surface2)` so they theme. New `.dash-card` class adds the elevation shadow + hover lift; `.dash-row` adds a slide-right hover (full-white fill on light). WIN/LOSS/TIE pills moved off inline colors to `.dash-pill--win/--loss/--tie` classes with theme-split palettes (light: deep red/green text on light tints; dark: light red/green on near-black tints). `.cta-ghost` light-theme override bumped from `rgba(0,0,0,.4)` text to `.72` + a faint white fill so "Open library" / "Open all" / "Analyze" read as real buttons. Dark theme untouched — only the `[data-lighting="light"]` block + the new shared classes changed. SW bumped to v508.

- **Brand renamed: DebateAI → Debatable** (2026-05-25). User acquired `debatable.com` and a few related domains. Visible brand surfaced across the site is now "Debatable" (titles, og/twitter, hero, topbar, footer, CTAs, JSON-LD `name` / `alternateName`, manifest `name`, body copy). What was deliberately NOT touched in this sweep: (a) the production domain `debateai.com` — still live in canonical URLs, og:url, sitemap entries, JSON-LD `url`, CORS allowlists, and mailto addresses. DNS migration is a separate move; flipping the domain in code before DNS would break canonical resolution + CORS overnight. (b) The Counter sub-brand at `app/extension/` — per the 2026-05-10 decision, the chrome extension ships as "Counter: Oral Exam Trainer (by Debatable)" and its own brand identity stays. The sweep updated the parenthetical attribution (was "by DebateAI") but left every other "Counter" string alone. (c) Historical decision-log entries in soul.md / AGENTS.md — those are timestamped events that describe what was true at the time; rewriting them would falsify the record. (d) Filenames like `debate-ai.html`, `debate-ai-tools.html`, `ai-debate-practice.html` — these are routes the rest of the codebase + Google's index reference; renaming them is a slug migration with redirects, not a brand rename. Net: 107 files touched, ~1230 visible mentions flipped, single commit on `chore/rename-to-debatable`. Hardcoded "Debate AI" / "DebateAI" → "Debatable" across both word forms via sed; the keyword meta-tag now shows `Debatable, Debatable, debateai.com` (harmless duplicate from the two source forms collapsing) — Google deprecated meta keywords years ago, low priority to deduplicate. Open follow-ups for the user, in rough priority order: (i) decide when to flip DNS on `debatable.com` and run a domain-migration sweep covering canonical URLs / og:url / sitemap / JSON-LD `url` / CORS allowlists / mailto; (ii) set up `feedback@debatable.com` and re-do the email sweep; (iii) update social handles (`tiktok.com/@debateai`, `instagram.com/debateai`, `twitter.com/debateai`, `linkedin.com/company/debateai`, `youtube.com/@debateai` are still old-brand in the `sameAs` JSON-LD); (iv) art/icon updates if "Debatable" wants a different logomark; (v) GSC: add `debatable.com` as a property once DNS resolves and submit the new sitemap.

---

- **Interim three-part naming system adopted, then superseded** (2026-05-26). The product briefly used one name in titles, “Debatable” as a personality word, and a two-word CTA slogan after discovering that `debatable.com` had not been acquired. That system is historical only. The 2026-07-25 decision makes **Debatable** the sole public brand at `itsdebatable.com`; old aliases and slogans must not return to visible copy, metadata, accessibility text, narration, or internal authoring guidance.

- **Expansive styles: Karl Popper + the Career trio (Courtroom / Negotiation / Pitch Defense)** (2026-06-10). Shipped the day before the Entrepreneur First meeting to make the "one engine, several markets" pitch visible in the product itself. Until now the format picker was 11 keys, all competitive prep except Viva; the professional vertical (lawyers / sales / founders) existed only as landing copy. Four new end-to-end formats: (1) **Karl Popper** ('popper') — the IDEA-circuit 3v3 prepared-resolution format dominant in Eastern Europe, the Balkans, the Caucasus, and Central Asia; 8 segments including two cross-ex periods, criterion-based judging, open citations (prepared topics mean real research is expected). Strengthens the global-circuit story next to Asian Parli / WSDC / BP. (2) **Courtroom** ('courtroom') — opening statements, cross-examination exchanges, closing arguments; AI plays opposing counsel; voice block encodes openings-preview-closings-argue, leading-question discipline, burden language, candor toward the tribunal, and a hard no-fabricated-case-law rule. (3) **Negotiation** ('negotiation') — anchor / counter-anchor, two objection rounds, concession trading, final offer, accept-or-walk; voice block encodes interests-over-positions, trade-never-give, walk-away realism. (4) **Pitch Defense** ('pitch') — 3-minute pitch then three investor pushes (market / moat / traction) and a close; voice block encodes the partner-meeting register: one push per turn, follow-the-dodge, vanity-metric allergy, reward candor with harder questions. The founder can literally rehearse an investor meeting on the product before walking into one. Client (`app/practice.html`): 4 FORMATS entries, 4 SEAT_LAYOUTS, both format-picker arrays extended to 15 keys, new blue 'Career' pill badge (`entry: 'career'`; blue = info, amber stays reserved for state per 2026-05-19), simple-mode gets a fifth "Work & Career" practice card routing to Pitch Defense. Server (`app/netlify/functions/lib/voice-guidelines.mjs`): 4 new FORMAT_VOICES blocks (14 → 18 keys) + forFormat synonyms ('karl popper', 'mock trial', 'moot court', 'sales', 'investor pitch', 'salary negotiation', etc.). Root `netlify/functions/lib/` mirror no longer exists on main, so the app copy is the single edit target. Stale "10 formats / ten formats" copy swept to 15 across debate-it (og + JSON-LD), story, future, online-debate-camp, debate-an-ai, uwc, landing infoRow, and both compare pages. Voice-debate drill MODES deliberately untouched — that surface's mode wiring is separate plumbing and the typed surface was the right scope for one pass. Live-round JUDGE_RULES also untouched (human-vs-human surface, classic formats only for now).

- **Contact email → founder Gmail sitewide + mailto chooser** (2026-07-04, Aidan's explicit call). REVERSES the 2026-06-14 email reversal: all user-facing contact points now read aidandavidhollinger@gmail.com; contact@devilsadvocateteam.com is retired everywhere in the repo (last refs were ambassadors.html + benchmark.html; most of the site had already been swept in earlier July sessions). The Google Workspace mailbox behind devilsadvocateteam.com is being cancelled (owner action), so any stale reference would BOUNCE; do not reintroduce it, and do not re-sweep back. Note: this knowingly supersedes the no-founder-name-on-site rule for the email address itself (his email contains his name); Aidan chose this. Also shipped: a mailto chooser in js/topbar.js — clicking any mailto link on the ~60 topbar pages now opens a small picker (Open in Gmail / Open in Outlook / Use my mail app / Copy address) instead of dumping visitors into whatever desktop client the OS defaults to; pages without topbar.js keep plain mailto.

- **Lifecycle email system unified: shared lib, real unsubscribe, deliverability, motion rotation** (2026-07-05). The five Resend senders (scheduled-wau-digest, scheduled-winback, send-first-round-email, notify-accepted, notify-dm-accept) each hand-rolled their own esc(), HTML shell, footer, and Resend fetch, with three different FROM defaults (aidandavidhollinger@gmail.com in three places, onboarding@resend.dev in two), no plain-text parts, no actionable unsubscribe ("Reply to opt out" was a manual flow), and inconsistent opt-out checks. Now: (1) new `lib/email.mjs` owns esc/toText/brandHeader/renderFooter/isOptedOut/unsubToken/verifyUnsubToken/unsubUrl/sendEmail; from resolves arg > EMAIL_FROM > 'Aidan at the retired brand <aidan@debateai.com>', per-function env overrides still honored; every send now carries a text/plain alternative. (2) Real unsubscribe: new `email-unsub.mjs` at /api/email-unsub, HMAC-SHA256-gated (EMAIL_UNSUB_SECRET, set on debateos1 2026-07-05), GET renders a branded confirmation page with undo + unsubscribe-from-all links, POST is RFC 8058 one-click; bulk sends (digest/winback/onboarding) carry List-Unsubscribe + List-Unsubscribe-Post headers, which Gmail/Yahoo bulk-sender rules require and which surfaces Gmail's native unsubscribe chip. Streams: digest -> wauDigestOptOut, winback -> winbackOptOut, all/onboarding -> emailOptOut (new global kill switch respected by every sender; winback keeps its legacy wauDigestOptOut||winbackOptOut behavior). Transactional sends (notify-accepted, notify-dm-accept) are exempt from bulk headers by design. (3) Digest motion bug fixed: MOTD_BY_FORMAT keys never matched the real generations format slugs (wsdc vs worlds, quickclash vs quick/clash/1v1 aliases), so every user got the same static fallback motion forever; keys realigned with a lowercase alias map and the fallback now rotates weekly via dailyMotionFor(run date) from lib/daily-motion-bank.mjs. (4) Escaping fixes riding along: previously-raw firstName in the digest H1 and firstName/side/format in the first-round email now esc'd; an em-dash in the notify-accepted headline removed. Root netlify/functions mirror confirmed VESTIGIAL for these files (no lib/ dir, imports could never resolve; app/netlify/functions is the deployed dir per both netlify.toml) but the two existing root copies were text-synced per the mirror convention. Env note: RESEND_API_KEY lives in the Netlify production context only, which is why `netlify env:list` does not show it; EMAIL_UNSUB_SECRET was added in the all context. No client files touched, so no SW bump.

- **Open Spar Night: fixed weekly live hour** (2026-07-15). The /spar liquidity problem is arrival-time spread, not traffic volume: on a small base, visitors almost never overlap inside the 60s matchmaking window, so the queue reads dead even in weeks with enough total traffic for ten rounds. Fix = concentrate demand at one fixed hour: **Wednesdays 8:00 PM ET, 90 minutes, first event 2026-07-22.** (The Des Moines cluster, the only organic liquidity to date, was already a cohort on a weekly schedule; this formalizes the mechanic, chess.com-arena style.) Shipped: `app/js/spar-night.js`, a deterministic client-side countdown (DST-safe America/New_York math, no server dependency) mounted on the landing #live-now band and the /spar waitlist rail via `data-spar-night` slots; flips to a green LIVE state during 8:00-9:30 PM ET with a "Join the queue" CTA, pre-event CTA is a recurring Google Calendar add (deliberately not a fake RSVP), GA4 `spar_night_seen` / `spar_night_click` events, `?sparnight=off` QA escape. Email: new `scheduled-spar-night.mjs` cron (Wed 13:00 UTC) emails every opted-in profile the morning of the event; dry-run unless `SPAR_NIGHT_ENABLED=1` (winback posture), only acts when the next event starts <24h out (so it stays quiet until event week), stamps `sparNightSentAt`; new `sparnight` unsub stream through lib/email.mjs + email-unsub.mjs; scheduled-winback (Wed 16:00 UTC) skips anyone spar-night-emailed the same day, preserving the one-email-per-day rule. Honesty guardrail: the live card says "Spar Night is on," never claims a full queue. Founders + first ambassadors seed the first events. ACTION: set `SPAR_NIGHT_ENABLED=1` in Netlify env before Jul 22, or the first event's email is a dry run.

- **Spar style picker + judge-paradigm notes with opponent consent** (2026-06-12). /spar's format was URL-param-only (default Quick Clash) and the AI judge ran one fixed lens for everyone. Now the searching screen carries a "Round setup" card: 8 style chips (Quick Clash / APDA / BP / Worlds / Asian Parli / LD / PF / Policy — the live-round-supported set; the Career trio stays on the AI surface) plus an optional 240-char "Judge instructions" note ("Weigh probability before magnitude. Punish drops hard."). Style switch = clean requeue (fresh doc, fresh 60s clock, URL + localStorage rewritten); the note rides the queue doc and persists across sessions. **Consent is the load-bearing piece:** a note never reaches the judge without both debaters' eyes on it. When either side of a candidate pair filed one, `spar-pair.mjs` lands the pair in a two-phase `status:'consent'` state instead of matching — both clients render an accept/pass card showing the opponent's exact note (20s auto-pass; a side with nothing to review is auto-consented; the proposer waits with a withdraw button + a silent 30s dead-peer net). Accept-by-both flips to 'matched' and collapses the notes into a name-attributed `pairedParadigm`; pass reverts both docs to 'waiting' with mutual `skipUids` (no re-propose loop, joinedAt refreshed so nobody gets stale-skipped for time spent reading) and a "{name} passed on the proposal" line on the other side. Background "Spar live" pairs (js/notifications.js has no consent surface) complete instantly and drop the notes rather than ride unconsented. Reaper sweeps stranded consent docs on the same (status, joinedAt) index; no Firestore rules changes (consent flips are all admin-SDK writes; the note lives on the user's own doc). On /live-round the agreed paradigm arrives by URL, renders under the format chips ("Judge paradigm · agreed by both"), and spreads into BOTH ballot builders as an AGREED JUDGE PARADIGM block layered on top of the adjudication core: it may shift emphasis (weighing order, speed tolerance, drop severity), it may NOT override deciding on the flow, and any instruction that names a winner or dictates scores is explicitly dead on arrival — the consent gate plus that guard is the prompt-injection defense. Anon /spar visitors get the same setup card (choices persist locally and apply on their first signed-in queue); switchFormat routes them through joinQueueAnon so the queue-less searching UI never dead-ends. **Hardening pass, same day** (after walking every join/crash/blip permutation as hostile QA): (a) ghost detection — tab close no longer deletes queue docs (the 0188953 queue-follows-you change), so proposing to a dead tab is routine; timer-fired declines now carry `auto:true` and when the peer never acted on a 25s+ proposal the server CANCELS the ghost (`cancelReason:'consent_ghost'`, which the client treats as a system cancel and auto-requeues if it was actually alive) instead of reverting it to 'waiting' with a fresh joinedAt that dressed a corpse up as the youngest doc in the queue. (b) Consent clicks got their own throttle lane — they previously shared the 600ms pair-poll throttle, so an Accept landing within 600ms of a poll POST got eaten by a 429 and the card sat dead; any failed consent POST now restores a usable card from the last snapshot. (c) The AI-fallback clock yields while a proposal is in flight (15s grace, keeps ticking rather than dying silently at 0:00) and triggerAIFallback hard-guards on inConsent. (d) Phase-1 proposals re-stamp joinedAt so the reaper sweeps a dead pair together, never one half. (e) The "{name} passed" notice holds 7s against tryMatch repaints; a tab-title nudge fires while a 20s decision is pending.

- **Lifetime tier removed from all pricing displays** (2026-07-03). The public pricing surface is Free, BYOK, Individual, and Team. Existing backend lifetime entitlements remain valid for accounts that already hold one, but the plan no longer appears in user-facing pricing, upsells, comparison pages, or legal copy.

- **Live voice upgraded to gpt-realtime-2.1 + reasoning effort** (2026-07-09). OpenAI shipped `gpt-realtime-2.1` and `gpt-realtime-2.1-mini` on 2026-07-06 (~25% lower p95 voice latency via better caching, steadier interruption handling, better alphanumeric/silence handling, and a new configurable `reasoning: { effort }` on the session — `minimal|low|medium|high|xhigh`, default `low`). Endpoints and body shape are UNCHANGED from `gpt-realtime`, so this was a drop-in. All three Realtime minters now put `gpt-realtime-2.1` at the front of the model try-list with `gpt-realtime` / `gpt-realtime-2` / `gpt-4o-realtime-preview` as fallbacks: `realtime-session.mjs` (the /voice-debate opponent + /newvoice, which is the landing's primary "Adaptive voice" CTA), `coach-session.mjs`, and `room-judge-session.mjs`. Reasoning effort is attached in the GA mint body ONLY when the negotiated model matches `^gpt-realtime-2\.1` (older models 400 on the field, which would drop us to the legacy path); the server also returns `reasoningEffort` so the client mirrors it in its post-connect `session.update` (guarded — never sent to a model that lacks it, since a bad field 400s the whole update and would drop turn_detection, the mid-speech-interruption feature). Default effort is `low` to keep the realtime path snappy; the main opponent nudges to `medium` on the top smartness tiers (World Champ / Council), and everything is tunable without redeploy via `OPENAI_REALTIME_REASONING_EFFORT` (plus `OPENAI_REALTIME_MODEL=gpt-realtime-2.1-mini` to trade depth for the cheaper $10/$20 vs $32/$64 audio pricing). Training-log `model` labels on the voice paths now record the actually-negotiated model instead of the hardcoded `gpt-realtime`. Verified: all three functions pass `node --check`, both inline-React and plain-JS client blocks parse. AGENTS.md's "OpenAI Realtime API reference" section + file map updated with the 2.1 models, the reasoning-effort field, and the new env var. SW cache auto-bumped by the pre-commit hook (client files touched: voice-debate.html, newvoice.html, practice.html).

- **Learning loop wired into the live voice opponent + newvoice turn-taking tuned** (2026-07-09). Two things, same push. (1) **Loop into voice.** The nightly learning loop (admin-weighted REFERENCE ROUNDS from `exemplars.mjs` + the per-format LEARNED PATTERNS distillation from `distillations.mjs`) fed the typed brains since 2026-05-13 but NOT the realtime voice path — so the live opponent captured every round into `generations` yet never read any back, and didn't compound. Now `realtime-session.mjs` pulls both, in parallel with the AI council (so session start pays max(), not sum), gated on feature `'opponent'` (the closest match in both helpers' allowlists — `'bot'` isn't in either) and keyed on `loopFormat = mode` so read ↔ capture stay symmetric. **Split by stack:** the full training stack (/voice-debate) gets BOTH exemplars + distillations; the deliberately-lean clash stack (/newvoice, `mode==='clash'`) gets distillations ONLY — full reference rounds would bloat the lean prompt and drag the model toward tournament-speak, which clash mode is built to avoid. Distillations are a compact pattern block, safe on both. Both helpers return '' gracefully until a doc exists, and work for anon callers, so the voice opponent now compounds from rated rounds the same way the typed side does. (2) **newvoice turn-taking**, per Aidan looking at a live round: response felt laggy and the AI interrupted too easily. newvoice runs a custom turn engine (`create_response:false` + `interrupt_response:false`, replies driven by a silence timer + spoken-handoff phrases, barge-in gated by a sustained-loudness meter). Dropped `TURN_SILENCE_MS` 3000 → 1500 (the AI now answers ~1.5s after you go quiet instead of 3s; handoff phrases still answer instantly), and decoupled the two mic thresholds that were conflated into one `BARGE_LEVEL`: a low `SPEECH_LEVEL` (0.12) keeps the user's turn alive / holds the AI off, while a higher `BARGE_LEVEL` (0.22) is what it now takes to cut the AI off mid-answer — so a cough or "mm" no longer steals the floor, only clearly-louder sustained speech does. voice-debate.html's separate server_vad path (silence_duration_ms via `vadSilenceMs(mode)`, interrupt_response:true) was left as-is; this tuning is newvoice-specific. Verified: `realtime-session.mjs` passes `node --check`, newvoice's inline script parses. SW cache auto-bumped (client file touched: newvoice.html).

- **Leaderboard seeds reintroduced — as Firestore docs this time** (2026-07-18, Aidan's call, REVERSES the 2026-05-25 "real entries only" decision). With the landing's new ranked band surfacing the leaderboard on the scroll, Aidan asked to fill the near-empty board (1 real entry) with believable seed data so the surface reads alive. 48 docs written directly to `leaderboard_entries` (ids `seed_v2_01..48`, fields `seed:true, seedV:2`) via the Firestore REST API — NOT the old client-side `community-seed.js` merge, which was removed 2026-05-25 and stays removed. Doc shape mirrors real entries (uid / displayName first-name+last-initial / score as 30-scale speaker points 26.3-29.5 / won / kind live-or-voice / format + formatName / completedAt recency-skewed minutes-to-3-weeks), so both /leaderboard (whose `seed:true` render path with synthetic W/L + streak texture survived the 2026-05-25 removal) and the landing band's /api/leaderboard-top display them with zero code changes.
